motionEvent.getHistorySize() > 0 ? Math.round(motionEvent.getHistoricalY(0)) : intCurrentY;\n            int intStartX = motionEvent.getHistorySize() > 0 ? Math.round(motionEvent.getHistoricalX(0)) : intCurrentX;\n\n            // How much you need to move your finger to be considered "moving"\n            int MOVE_SENSITIVITY = 8;\n            boolean hasMoved = (Math.abs(intCurrentX - intStartX) > MOVE_SENSITIVITY) || (Math.abs(intCurrentY - intStartY) > MOVE_SENSITIVITY);\n\n            if (hasMoved && mDragEnabled && !isDragging) {\n DragEvent.ACTION_DRAG_ENTERED:\n                return isDragging;\n            case DragEvent.ACTION_DRAG_LOCATION:\n                ViewGroup bar = ((ViewGroup) targetView);\n                float x bar.getChildAt(currentPos);\n                if (currentChildAtPos != draggedView) {\n                    // Do not update the ViewGroup during the event dispatch, older Androids will crash\n                    // See #1419\n                    bar.removeView(draggedView);\n                    try {\n                        bar.addView(draggedView, currentPos);\n                    } catch (IllegalStateException e) {\n                        // In some situations,\n                        // removeView() somehow fails (this especially happens if you start the drag and immediately moves to the left or right)\n                        // and we can\'t add the children back, because it still has a parent. but also removes all animation.\n                        potentialNewIndex = -1;\n                        return false;\n                    }\n                }\n\n                potentialNewIndex = currentPos;\n                return true;\n            case DragEvent.ACTION_DROP:\n                // Accept the drop, will be followed by ACTION_DRAG_ENDED\n                return isDragging;\n starting position");\n                            // We still need to refresh our favorites, in case one was removed and never added back (see IllegalStateException above)\n                            mainActivity.onFavoriteChange();\n                        } else {\n                            // Signals to a View that the drag and drop operation has concluded.\n                            // If event result is set, this means the dragged view was dropped in target\n                            if (event.getResult()) {\n                                KissApplication.getApplication(mainActivity).getDataHandler().setFavoritePosition(mainActivity, draggedApp.result.getPojoId(), newIndex);\n                            }\n                        }\n                    } catch (IllegalStateException e) {\n                        // An animation was running. Retry later\n                        // (to trigger: long press a favorite while already moving your finger a little, release as soon as you get haptic feedback)\n                        draggedView.postDelayed(mainActivity::onFavoriteChange, 300);\n                    }\n                });\n\n                mDragEnabled = favCount > 1;\n                potentialNewIndex = -1;\n                isDragging = false;\n                return true;\n            default:\n                break;\n        }\n        return isDragging;\n    }\n\n\n    /**\n     * On first run, fill the AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='## Summary\n\n**Root cause** (`app/src/main/java/fr/neamar/kiss/forwarder/ExperienceTweaks.java`)\n\nThe only code that un-hides the favorites bar lived inside `doAction("display-history")`:\n\n```java\ncase "display-history":\n    if (isMinimalisticModeEnabled()) { ...show history... }\n    if (isMinimalisticModeEnabledForFavorites()) {\n        mainActivity.favoritesBar.setVisibility(View.VISIBLE);   // only reachable here\n    }\n```\n\nand on a screen tap `doAction("display-history")` was only invoked when the **separate** `history-onclick` ("Show history on touch") preference was enabled:\n\n```java\nif (!prefs.getBoolean("double-tap", false)) {\n    if (prefs.getBoolean("history-onclick", false)) {   // ← gate\n        doAction("display-history");\n    }\n}\n```\n\nMeanwhile, `favorites-hide` ("Hide favorites bar initially") is an independent switch that only depends on `history-hide`. So a user who enables Minimalistic UI + "Hide favorites bar initially" *without* "Show history on touch" gets a bar that is hidden at startup (by `updateSearchRecords`/`onDisplayKissBar`) and can **never** be brought back — exactly the reported symptom, and contrary to the project\'s own QA suite ("Minimalistic mode on for favorites → Touching the screen on an empty area displays history and the favorites").\n\n**Fix**\n\n- Extracted the tap handling into a single `onTap()` used by both `onSingleTapUp` (double-tap-to-lock off) and `onSingleTapConfirmed` (double-tap-to-lock on), so behavior is identical in both modes.\n- `onTap()` now reveals the hidden favorites bar regardless of `history-onclick`: if history-on-touch is enabled it delegates to `doAction("display-history")` (which reveals the bar as before), otherwise it reveals the bar directly.\n- Added the small helper `displayHiddenFavoritesBar()` (guarded by the existing `isMinimalisticModeEnabledForFavorites()`, so nothing changes for users who don\'t hide the bar or who use the embedded bar) and reused it in `doAction` to keep a single source of truth.\n\nNo behavior changes for any other setting combination: the gestures, history-on-touch, double-tap-to-lock and embedded-bar paths all keep their previous semantics.
